HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000713/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3ae8f84f451c3777c0a6","text":"3. **Capture at the bodily point of control.** `س ف ع:B001` supplies coercive seizure by the forelock, and `ن ص ي:B001` supplies both the forelock and figurative control through taking it. Their immediate combination removes lexical ambiguity: one root supplies the force and the other the handle and control relation. I infer that bodily capture enacts loss of agency rather than merely accompanying it. The threat thus reads as a concrete reversal of the antagonist’s effort to control another’s worship.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001560/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3d6ed196a7544cf05479","text":"2. **An externally supplied endpoint.** `ن ه ي:B002` supplies the endpoint, edge, or limit reached by a thing. The conditional says that if the actor does not bring his conduct to an end, forelock seizure follows. The endpoint branch makes the threat functionally exact: refusal to stop internally produces an imposed terminus. The packet supplies the conditional sequence; I infer the causal arrow from non-cessation to enforced limit. `ينته` therefore concerns both moral cessation and who controls the action’s endpoint.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001560/B001","root_001560/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_41fd1bde6ff09c96f474","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:15:4\",\"root_001560\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kişi kendi isteğiyle son vermezse davranışına dışarıdan zorunlu bir bitiş konur; artık eylemin sonunu kimin belirleyeceği söz konusudur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001512/B001","root_001512/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5b4bc9018cd416b06b08","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:15:6\",\"root_001512\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Perçem, kişinin başıyla birlikte topluluğa dayalı iktidarının ön safını da temsil eder; yakalama tehdidi bu saygınlık düzenini önder noktasından çökertir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001512/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5d82244fdf449adb976a","text":"4. **Seizing the social vanguard as well as the head.** `ن ص ي:B003` supplies the chosen elite, noble leaders, or front rank. The next lines characterize the forelock, then let the actor summon his council (96:16–17); a counter-summons follows in 96:18. The elite branch makes the forelock the social “front” of his authority, not only hair. I infer metonymic extension from bodily front to leadership. The threat can therefore announce collapse of a whole prestige structure at its leading point.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000713/B001","root_000713/B002","root_000713/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_742f5929ce028300a1c5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:15:5\",\"root_000713\",[\"B001\",\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yakalama fiilinin karartma ve kavurma çağrışımları, zorla ele geçirmenin aynı zamanda suçu bedende görünür kılan kamusal bir damga olabileceğini düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001560/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7d7876db75679eb13cae","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:15:4\",\"root_001560\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Başkalarını durduran kişi bu kez durması gereken kişi yapılır; aynı sınırlama kökü haksız engeli sahibine geri çevirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001560/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_844e25f3d6ba9d64f34f","text":"1. **The restrainer made the one who must cease.** `ن ه ي:B001` supplies both restraining another and ceasing oneself. The earlier finite roles reverse across 96:9 and 96:15: the one who `ينهى` a servant is now the subject of `لم ينته`. The root’s role is juridical symmetry, turning his imposed restraint back upon him as a required self-restraint. The packet supplies the morphological recurrence; I infer that the reversal is corrective. The line reads as a transfer of the stopping command from the servant to the controller.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000713/B002","root_001512/B001","root_001560/B001","root_001583/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a7ebdbb6070ef15b76ac","text":"1. The wrongful restrainer is commanded to reach his endpoint or be seized by the front. Evidence: `ن ه ي B001` ceasing/restraint, `ن ه ي B002` endpoint/limit, `س ف ع B001` seizing by forelock, `ن ص ي B001` forelock and taking by it. Structural cue: `لم ينته` reverses 96:9 `ينهى`; the one who forbade must now desist. Mechanism: illegitimate restraint triggers legitimate coercive restraint at the body's leading point. Abductive move: the packet supplies the root reversal and the forelock seizure branches; I infer retaliatory symmetry. Reading change: the threat reads as a precise reversal of his action, not generic punishment. 2. `نسفعاً` also activates discoloration/scorching, turning moral exposure into a visible mark. Evidence: `س ف ع B002` reddish blackness, `س ف ع B003` scorching by fire/hot wind, `ع ل م B002` mark/sign, `ر ء ي B006` visible appearance. Structural cue: 96:14 says Allah sees; 96:15 makes something visible on the front. Mechanism: hidden denial becomes a marked, darkened front. Abductive move: the packet gives color/scorch branches; I infer that seizing may include visible shaming. Reading change: the ayah reads both as physical grasp and as public marking/exposure. 3. The forelock activates control over direction. Evidence: `ن ص ي B001` figurative control by taking the forelock; `ه د ي B003` leading front part; `و ل ي B006/B007` turning toward/away. Structural cue: the one who turned away is threatened at the front of movement. Mechanism: taking the forelock arrests the body's false direction and reassigns control. Abductive move: the packet supplies front/leading and turning branches; I infer a directional-control mechanism. Reading change: `بالناصية` becomes the point where misguided agency is physically overruled.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001512/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ae54496718a61d2f6326","text":"96:16 made the threatened forelock a morally qualified locus of agency rather than a neutral body-part. 96:17–18 then activated `ن ص ي:B003`: the seized “front” is also the leading edge of a council-based power formation answered by another force. Finally, 96:19’s voluntary lowering completed the reversal between imposed capture and chosen submission.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b35fbc759f480450f196","text":"96:19 made the forelock seizure contrast with voluntary prostration: one head/front is dragged down by force, the other lowers itself in obedience.
